  onu profile sip SIP-VOIP-GOLDNET proxy 179.189.93.88 outbound 179.189.93.88 registrar 179.189.93.88
  onu profile sip SIP-VIVO proxy 187.50.251.58 outbound 187.50.251.58 registrar metapabx.vivo.net.br
  onu profile sip SIP-TRIX proxy pabx.telefonefixodigital.com outbound pabx.telefonefixodigital.com registrar pabx.telefonefixodigital.com

  onu profile vlan 54 tag-mode tag cvlan 54
  onu profile vlan 711 tag-mode tag cvlan 711
  onu profile vlan 811 tag-mode tag cvlan 811
  onu profile vlan 813 tag-mode tag cvlan 813
  onu profile vlan 911 tag-mode tag cvlan 911
  profile tcont 1G type 4 maximum 1024000
  profile tcont SMARTOLT-VOIPMNG-10M type 5 fixed 512 assured 1024 maximum 11264
  profile tcont SMARTOLT-1G-UP type 5 fixed 64 assured 64 maximum 1048064
